--  DDL for Trigger STEP_BIR
--------------------------------------------------------

  CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER "KUEHLEGACY"."STEP_BIR" 
BEFORE INSERT ON STEPS 
FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
    SELECT step_seq.NEXTVAL
    INTO :new.STEPID
    FROM dual;
END;

/
ALTER TRIGGER "KUEHLEGACY"."STEP_BIR" ENABLE;
--------------------------------------------------------
--  DDL for Trigger TRG_CHECK_KUEH_USERNAME
--------------------------------------------------------

  CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER "KUEHLEGACY"."TRG_CHECK_KUEH_USERNAME" 
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON KUEH
FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE
    v_count NUMBER;
BEGIN
    -- Check if the USERNAME exists in the USERS table
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
    FROM USERS
    WHERE USERNAME = :NEW.USERNAME;

    -- If not found in USERS, check the ADMIN table
    IF v_count = 0 THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count
        FROM ADMIN
        WHERE USERNAME = :NEW.USERNAME;
    END IF;

    -- If the USERNAME does not exist in either table, raise an error
    IF v_count = 0 THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001, 'USERNAME does not exist in USERS or ADMIN table.');
    END IF;
END;


/
ALTER TRIGGER "KUEHLEGACY"."TRG_CHECK_KUEH_USERNAME" ENABLE;
--------------------------------------------------------
--  DDL for Trigger TRG_FAVORITE_PK
--------------------------------------------------------

  CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER "KUEHLEGACY"."TRG_FAVORITE_PK" 
BEFORE INSERT ON favorite
FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
    IF :NEW.favID IS NULL THEN
        :NEW.favID := seq_favorite_id.NEXTVAL;
    END IF;
END;
